/**
 * Room Response Simulation Benchmark
 * 
 * This is a simplified, purely sequential implementation of room impulse response
 * simulation using radiosity-based wave propagation. It models how sound/light
 * waves propagate between surfaces in a room by computing:
 * 
 * 1. Form factors (Kij) between all pairs of triangles based on visibility and geometry
 * 2. Wave propagation using radiosity equations with time delays (Tau)
 * 3. Distance estimation via cross-correlation of radiosity values
 * 
 * The implementation generates a procedural icosphere mesh to simulate a room.
 */

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <map>
#include <memory>
#include <random>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// ============================================================================
// Types and Constants
// ============================================================================

using idx_t = uint32_t;
using val_t = float;

constexpr val_t PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;
constexpr val_t ZERO = 0.0f;
constexpr val_t ONE = 1.0f;
constexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)
constexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;
constexpr int NUM_RAYS = 16;              // Number of rays for visibility sampling
constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;

// ============================================================================
// Vector and Triangle Types
// ============================================================================

struct Vec3 {
    val_t x, y, z;

    constexpr Vec3() : x(0), y(0), z(0) {}
    constexpr Vec3(val_t x, val_t y, val_t z) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
    explicit constexpr Vec3(val_t v) : x(v), y(v), z(v) {}

    Vec3 operator+(const Vec3& o) const { return {x + o.x, y + o.y, z + o.z}; }
    Vec3 operator-(const Vec3& o) const { return {x - o.x, y - o.y, z - o.z}; }
    Vec3 operator*(val_t s) const { return {x * s, y * s, z * s}; }
    Vec3 operator/(val_t s) const { return {x / s, y / s, z / s}; }
    Vec3 operator-() const { return {-x, -y, -z}; }

    val_t dot(const Vec3& o) const { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }
    Vec3 cross(const Vec3& o) const {
        return {y * o.z - z * o.y, z * o.x - x * o.z, x * o.y - y * o.x};
    }

    val_t squaredNorm() const { return x * x + y * y + z * z; }
    val_t norm() const { return std::sqrt(squaredNorm()); }
    Vec3 normalized() const {
        val_t n = norm();
        return n > EPSILON ? *this / n : Vec3();
    }

    bool operator==(const Vec3& o) const {
        return std::abs(x - o.x) < EPSILON && std::abs(y - o.y) < EPSILON && std::abs(z - o.z) < EPSILON;
    }
};

struct Triangle {
    Vec3 a, b, c;
    Vec3 _normal;

    Triangle() = default;
    Triangle(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b, const Vec3& c)
        : a(a), b(b), c(c), _normal((b - a).cross(c - a).normalized()) {}

    Vec3 center() const { return (a + b + c) / 3.0f; }
    Vec3 normal() const { return _normal; }

    val_t area() const {
        Vec3 ab = b - a;
        Vec3 ac = c - a;
        return 0.5f * ab.cross(ac).norm();
    }

    bool operator==(const Triangle& o) const { return a == o.a && b == o.b && c == o.c; }
};

// ============================================================================
// Triangle-Box Overlap Test (for octree construction)
// ============================================================================

// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap
bool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3& boxHalfSize, const Triangle& tri) {
    // Translate triangle to box center
    Vec3 v0 = tri.a - boxCenter;
    Vec3 v1 = tri.b - boxCenter;
    Vec3 v2 = tri.c - boxCenter;

    // Triangle edges
    Vec3 e0 = v1 - v0;
    Vec3 e1 = v2 - v1;
    Vec3 e2 = v0 - v2;

    // Test box normals (AABB axes)
    auto minMax3 = [](val_t a, val_t b, val_t c) {
        return std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));
    };

    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);
    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return false;

    auto [minY, maxY] = minMax3(v0.y, v1.y, v2.y);
    if (minY > boxHalfSize.y || maxY < -boxHalfSize.y) return false;

    auto [minZ, maxZ] = minMax3(v0.z, v1.z, v2.z);
    if (minZ > boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;

    // Test triangle normal
    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);
    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);
    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +
              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +
              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);
    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;

    // Test 9 edge cross products
    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {
        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);
        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);
        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);
        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +
                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +
                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);
        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);
        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);
    };

    Vec3 axes[3] = {{1,0,0}, {0,1,0}, {0,0,1}};
    Vec3 edges[3] = {e0, e1, e2};
    for (const auto& axis : axes) {
        for (const auto& edge : edges) {
            Vec3 crossAxis = axis.cross(edge);
            if (crossAxis.squaredNorm() > EPSILON) {
                if (!testAxis(crossAxis)) return false;
            }
        }
    }

    return true;
}

// ============================================================================
// Octree for Spatial Acceleration
// ============================================================================

constexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;
constexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;

class Octree {
public:
    Vec3 minBound, maxBound;
    Vec3 halfExtent, center;
    std::unique_ptr<Octree> children[8];
    std::vector<size_t> triangleIndices;  // Indices into the global triangle list
    const std::vector<Triangle>* allTriangles;  // Pointer to all triangles

    Octree() : allTriangles(nullptr) {}

    void build(const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles) {
        allTriangles = &triangles;
        if (triangles.empty()) return;

        // Compute bounding box
        minBound = triangles[0].a;
        maxBound = triangles[0].a;
        for (const auto& tri : triangles) {
            for (const auto* v : {&tri.a, &tri.b, &tri.c}) {
                minBound.x = std::min(minBound.x, v->x);
                minBound.y = std::min(minBound.y, v->y);
                minBound.z = std::min(minBound.z, v->z);
                maxBound.x = std::max(maxBound.x, v->x);
                maxBound.y = std::max(maxBound.y, v->y);
                maxBound.z = std::max(maxBound.z, v->z);
            }
        }

        // Collect all indices
        std::vector<size_t> allIndices(triangles.size());
        for (size_t i = 0; i < triangles.size(); ++i) allIndices[i] = i;

        buildNode(allIndices, minBound, maxBound);
    }

private:
    void buildNode(const std::vector<size_t>& indices, const Vec3& nodeMin, const Vec3& nodeMax) {
        minBound = nodeMin;
        maxBound = nodeMax;
        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;
        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;

        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf
        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||
            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {
            triangleIndices = indices;
            return;
        }

        // Subdivide into 8 children
        Vec3 childHalfSize = halfExtent * 0.5f;
        std::vector<size_t> childIndices[8];

        for (size_t idx : indices) {
            const Triangle& tri = (*allTriangles)[idx];

            // Check which children this triangle overlaps
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
                Vec3 childCenter = center;
                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;
                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;
                childCenter.z += (i & 4) ? childHalfSize.z : -childHalfSize.z;

                if (triangleBoxOverlap(childCenter, childHalfSize, tri)) {
                    childIndices[i].push_back(idx);
                }
            }
        }

        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)
        bool canSplit = false;
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            if (!childIndices[i].empty() && childIndices[i].size() < indices.size()) {
                canSplit = true;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (!canSplit) {
            triangleIndices = indices;
            return;
        }

        // Build children
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            if (!childIndices[i].empty()) {
                Vec3 childMin = center;
                Vec3 childMax = center;
                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)

                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;
                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : minBound.y;
                childMax.y = (i & 2) ? maxBound.y : center.y;
                childMin.z = (i & 4) ? center.z : minBound.z;
                childMax.z = (i & 4) ? maxBound.z : center.z;

                children[i] = std::make_unique<Octree>();
                children[i]->allTriangles = allTriangles;
                children[i]->buildNode(childIndices[i], childMin, childMax);
            }
        }
    }

public:
    // Check if a ray intersects this node's bounding box
    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {
        Vec3 d = (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;
        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;
        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};

        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;
        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;
        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;

        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;
        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;
        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;

        return true;
    }

    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray
    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle
    template<typename Func>
    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {
        // If leaf node, check triangles directly
        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {
            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {
                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        // Otherwise, descend to children
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {
                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
};

// ============================================================================
// Mesh Generation: Icosphere
// ============================================================================

// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron
class IcosphereMesh {
public:
    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;

    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {
        // Initial icosahedron vertices
        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;

        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {
            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius
        };

        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)
        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {
            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},
            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},
            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},
            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}
        };

        // Subdivide
        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {
            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;
            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;

            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {
                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));
                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);
                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;

                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;
                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;
                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());
                vertices.push_back(mid);
                midpointCache[key] = idx;
                return idx;
            };

            for (const auto& face : faces) {
                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);
                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);
                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);

                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});
                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});
                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});
                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});
            }
            faces = std::move(newFaces);
        }

        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a "room")
        triangles.reserve(faces.size());
        for (const auto& face : faces) {
            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward
            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);
        }
    }
};

// ============================================================================
// Random Number Generation
// ============================================================================

class RandomGenerator {
    std::mt19937 rng;
    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
public:
    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
};

// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates
Vec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {
    val_t u = rng.rand();
    val_t v = rng.rand();
    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
        u = 1.0f - u;
        v = 1.0f - v;
    }
    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;
    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;
    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;
}

// ============================================================================
// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)
// ============================================================================

val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,
                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {
    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;
    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;
    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);
    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);

    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;
    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;
    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;
    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);
    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;
    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;
}

// ============================================================================
// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)
// ============================================================================

// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle
// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search
bool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,
                  const Octree& octree,
                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {
    Vec3 dir = to - from;
    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();
    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;
    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;

    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {
        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;

        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);
        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {
            return true;  // Ray is blocked
        }
        return false;
    });
}

// ============================================================================
// Form Factor Computation (Kij)
// ============================================================================

// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal
val_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {
    val_t vNorm = v.norm();
    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;
    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);
}

// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)
val_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,
                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,
                  const Octree& octree,
                  RandomGenerator& rng) {
    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];
    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];

    // Cull triangles facing the same direction
    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;

    val_t kij = ZERO;

    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);
        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);

        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;

        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;
        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();
        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;

        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());
        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());

        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;

        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);
    }

    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;
}

// ============================================================================
// Tau (time delay) Computation
// ============================================================================

int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {
    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();
    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
}

// ============================================================================
// Room Response Simulation State
// ============================================================================

struct SimulationState {
    size_t numTriangles;
    size_t numTimesteps;

    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle
    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)
    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)
    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)
    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)
    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)
    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source

    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure

    size_t sourceIndex;

    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
};

// ============================================================================
// Initialization
// ============================================================================

void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {
    // Generate mesh
    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;

    printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);

    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
    printf("Building octree...\n");
    state.octree.build(state.triangles);

    // Initialize areas
    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
    }

    // Initialize reflectivity
    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);

    // Initialize matrices
    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);
    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);

    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)
    size_t timeOn = 0;
    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;
    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {
        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Precomputation Phase
// ============================================================================

void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
    RandomGenerator rng(42);

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
        }
        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
        }
    }
}

void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(
                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
// ============================================================================

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");

    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
            val_t sumB = ZERO;

            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                if (i == j) continue;

                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];

                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;

                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];
                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;

                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];
                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;

                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
            }

            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];
        }

        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
// ============================================================================

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
        int bestT = 0;

        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;

            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];
                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];
                sum += pS * pB;
            }

            if (sum > maxCorr) {
                maxCorr = sum;
                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
            }
        }

        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Validation
// ============================================================================

bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
    printf("\nValidation:\n");

    // Check that distances are non-negative
    bool allNonNegative = true;
    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t sumDist = ZERO;
    int nonZeroCount = 0;

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t d = state.distances[i];
        if (d < 0) {
            allNonNegative = false;
            printf("  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
        }
        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
            printf("  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
            return false;
        }
        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
        sumDist += d;
        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;
    }

    printf("  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\n", minDist, maxDist);
    printf("  Average distance: %.4f\n", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));
    printf("  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\n", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);

    // Check source distance is zero or very small
    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];
    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {
        printf("  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\n", srcDist);
    }

    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)
    int receivedEnergy = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {
                receivedEnergy++;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    printf("  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\n", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);

    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {
        printf("  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\n");
        return false;
    }

    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)
    int nonZeroKij = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) nonZeroKij++;
    }
    printf("  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\n",
           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,
           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));

    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {
        printf("  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\n");
        return false;
    }

    if (!allNonNegative) {
        return false;
    }

    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
    return true;
}

// ============================================================================
// Hash for Verification
// ============================================================================

uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
    uint64_t hash = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);
        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
    }
    return hash;
}

// ============================================================================
// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
// ============================================================================

int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision
    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480
    int subdivisions = 0;
    int triangles = 20;
    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {
        subdivisions++;
        triangles *= 4;
    }
    return subdivisions;
}

// ============================================================================
// Main
// ============================================================================

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;
    int sourceIdx = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);

    printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
    printf("===================================\n");
    printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
    printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
    printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
    printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    printf("\n");

    // Initialize
    SimulationState state;
    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);

    printf("\n");

    // Precomputation
    auto startPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeTimeDelays(state);
    computeFormFactors(state);

    auto endPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto preDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endPre - startPre).count();

    printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n", preDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Simulation
    auto startSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    runSimulation(state);

    auto endSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto simDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endSim - startSim).count();

    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", simDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Distance computation
    auto startDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeDistances(state);

    auto endDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto distDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endDist - startDist).count();

    printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n", distDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Total time
    long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
    printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);

    // Performance metrics
    size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);
    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);
    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);

    printf("\nPerformance:\n");
    printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
    printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
    printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
    printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
    printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
    printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);

    // Memory usage
    size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);
    size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;
    printf("  Memory usage: %.2f MB\n", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));

    // Hash
    uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);
    printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
    printf("\n");
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Convert distances to double for output
        std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());
        print_results(distData, "Distances");
    }

    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        if (!validateResults(state)) {
            return 1;
        }
    }

    return 0;
}

The main dependency pattern is favorable for a row decomposition: form factors and delays are independent by receiver triangle, while propagation only needs a compact all-gather of the newly completed radiosity row each timestep. I’m also preserving the sequential RNG stream per matrix row so results remain process-count invariant, rather than allowing rank-local random streams to change the physics.

        // current row is needed before ranks can advance to the next timestep.
        val_t* row = state.radB.data() + t * state.numTriangles;
        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,
                       row, state.recvCounts.data(), state.recvDisplacements.data(),
                       MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
// ============================================================================

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");

    for (size_t i = state.localBegin; i < state.localBegin + state.localCount; ++i) {
        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
        int bestT = 0;

        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;

            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];
                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];
                sum += pS * pB;
            }

            if (sum > maxCorr) {
                maxCorr = sum;
                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
            }
        }

        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
    }

    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,
                   state.distances.data(), state.recvCounts.data(),
                   state.recvDisplacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
}

// ============================================================================
// Validation
// ============================================================================

bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
    printf("\nValidation:\n");

    // Check that distances are non-negative
    bool allNonNegative = true;
    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t sumDist = ZERO;
    int nonZeroCount = 0;

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t d = state.distances[i];
        if (d < 0) {
            allNonNegative = false;
            printf("  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
        }
        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
            printf("  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
            return false;
        }
        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
        sumDist += d;
        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;
    }

    printf("  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\n", minDist, maxDist);
    printf("  Average distance: %.4f\n", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));
    printf("  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\n", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);

    // Check source distance is zero or very small
    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];
    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {
        printf("  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\n", srcDist);
    }

    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)
    int receivedEnergy = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {
                receivedEnergy++;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    printf("  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\n", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);

    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {
        printf("  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\n");
        return false;
    }

    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)
    int nonZeroKij = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) nonZeroKij++;
    }
    printf("  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\n",
           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,
           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));

    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {
        printf("  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\n");
        return false;
    }

    if (!allNonNegative) {
        return false;
    }

    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
    return true;
}

// ============================================================================
// Hash for Verification
// ============================================================================

uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
    uint64_t hash = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);
        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
    }
    return hash;
}

// ============================================================================
// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
// ============================================================================

int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision
    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480
    int subdivisions = 0;
    int triangles = 20;
    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {
        subdivisions++;
        triangles *= 4;
    }
    return subdivisions;
}

// ============================================================================
// Main
// ============================================================================

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;
    int sourceIdx = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);

    printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
    printf("===================================\n");
    printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
    printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
    printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
    printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    printf("\n");

    // Initialize
    SimulationState state;
    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);

    printf("\n");

    // Precomputation
    auto startPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeTimeDelays(state);
    computeFormFactors(state);

    auto endPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto preDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endPre - startPre).count();

    printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n", preDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Simulation
    auto startSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    runSimulation(state);

    auto endSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto simDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endSim - startSim).count();

    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", simDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Distance computation
    auto startDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeDistances(state);

    auto endDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto distDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endDist - startDist).count();

    printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n", distDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Total time
    long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
    printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);

    // Performance metrics
    size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);
    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);
    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);

    printf("\nPerformance:\n");
    printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
    printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
    printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
    printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
    printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
    printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);

    // Memory usage
    size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);
    size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;
    printf("  Memory usage: %.2f MB\n", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));

    // Hash
    uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);
    printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
    printf("\n");
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {

codex
The core implementation is now distributed: each rank stores only its balanced block of the dense form-factor/delay matrices, all ranks retain the time history needed for delayed propagation, and one in-place `MPI_Allgatherv` exchanges only \(N\) floats per timestep. Root-only reporting and collective validation avoid duplicated output, and phase timings now report the slowest rank.
The row decomposition, timestep collectives, distributed validation, and required MPI CMake linkage are implemented. Moving into build and process-count invariance testing.
  ✓ Inspect the roomsim code, build configuration, and available tests/inputs
  ✓ Design and implement the MPI decomposition and communication in existing files
  ✓ Update CMake to require and link MPI without renaming the executable
  → Build, run correctness checks at multiple process counts, and assess performance risks

codex
Correctness is process-count invariant so far: 1, 3, and 4 ranks reproduce the original 80-triangle result hash and byte-for-byte distance output; validation statistics also match. The 4-rank precomputation dropped from roughly 84 ms to 21 ms on this host, so I’m checking the larger default case and edge conditions next.

codex
The default-sized case exactly matches the original hash, and the MPI version scales from 1.499 s on one rank to 0.392 s on four ranks (about 3.8×), while maximum per-rank dense-state memory falls from 0.65 MB to 0.21 MB. I also verified ranks with zero owned rows by running 24 processes on 20 triangles; the collectives and validation remain correct.

Key implementation locations:
   515	int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {
   516	    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();
   517	    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
   518	}
   519	
   520	// ============================================================================
   521	// Room Response Simulation State
   522	// ============================================================================
   523	
   524	struct SimulationState {
   525	    size_t numTriangles;
   526	    size_t numTimesteps;
   527	
   528	    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
   529	    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle
   530	    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)
   531	    // The expensive N x N data is distributed by receiver row.  Coupling
   532	    // contains min(Kij * area[j], 1), which is the value used in every
   533	    // propagation timestep.
   534	    std::vector<val_t> coupling;    // localN x N matrix, row-major
   535	    std::vector<uint16_t> tau;      // localN x N matrix, row-major
   536	    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Replicated T x N history
   537	    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Replicated computed distances
   538	
   539	    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure
   540	
   541	    size_t sourceIndex;
   542	    size_t localBegin = 0;
   543	    size_t localCount = 0;
   544	    uint64_t localNonZeroKij = 0;
   545	    int rank = 0;
   546	    int worldSize = 1;
   547	    std::vector<int> recvCounts;
   548	    std::vector<int> recvDisplacements;
   549	
   550	    size_t idxLocal(size_t i, size_t j) const {
   551	        return (i - localBegin) * numTriangles + j;
   552	    }
   553	    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
   554	};
   555	
   556	// ============================================================================
   557	// Initialization
   558	// ============================================================================
   559	
   560	void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
   561	                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity,
   562	                          int rank, int worldSize) {
   563	    state.rank = rank;
   564	    state.worldSize = worldSize;
   565	
   566	    // Generate mesh
   567	    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
   568	    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
   569	    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
   570	    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
   571	    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;
   572	
   573	    if (rank == 0) {
   574	        printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);
   575	    }
   576	
   577	    // Contiguous, balanced row ownership keeps the collectives compact and
   578	    // gives each rank almost exactly the same O(N^2) work.
   579	    const size_t base = state.numTriangles / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
   580	    const size_t remainder = state.numTriangles % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
   581	    state.localCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   582	    state.localBegin = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
   583	                       std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
   584	
   585	    state.recvCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   586	    state.recvDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   587	    for (int r = 0; r < worldSize; ++r) {
   588	        const size_t rCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   589	        const size_t rBegin = static_cast<size_t>(r) * base +
   590	                              std::min(static_cast<size_t>(r), remainder);
   591	        state.recvCounts[static_cast<size_t>(r)] = static_cast<int>(rCount);
   592	        state.recvDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(r)] = static_cast<int>(rBegin);
   593	    }
   594	
   595	    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
   596	    if (rank == 0) printf("Building octree...\n");
   597	    state.octree.build(state.triangles);
   598	
   599	    // Initialize areas
   600	    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
   601	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   602	        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
   603	    }
   604	
   605	    // Initialize reflectivity
   606	    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);
   607	
   608	    // Initialize matrices
   609	    state.coupling.resize(state.localCount * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   610	    state.tau.resize(state.localCount * state.numTriangles, 0);
   611	    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   612	    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   613	}
   614	
   615	// ============================================================================
   616	// Precomputation Phase
   617	// ============================================================================
   618	
   619	void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
   620	    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
   621	    RandomGenerator rng(42);
   622	
   623	    // Every off-diagonal pair consumes exactly four random values per ray.
   624	    // Advancing to this rank's first row preserves the original global RNG
   625	    // stream, so output is identical for every MPI process count.
   626	    constexpr uint64_t randomValuesPerPair = 4ULL * NUM_RAYS;
   627	    const uint64_t precedingPairs = static_cast<uint64_t>(state.localBegin) *
   628	                                    (state.numTriangles - 1);
   629	    rng.discard(precedingPairs * randomValuesPerPair);
   630	
   631	    for (size_t i = state.localBegin; i < state.localBegin + state.localCount; ++i) {
   632	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   633	            if (i == j) continue;
   634	            const val_t kij = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
   635	            state.localNonZeroKij += kij > EPSILON ? 1 : 0;
   636	            state.coupling[state.idxLocal(i, j)] =
   637	                std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE);
   638	        }
   639	    }
   640	}
   641	
   642	void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
   643	    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");
   644	
   645	    for (size_t i = state.localBegin; i < state.localBegin + state.localCount; ++i) {
   646	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   647	            if (i == j) continue;
   648	            state.tau[state.idxLocal(i, j)] = static_cast<uint16_t>(computeTau(
   649	                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]));
   650	        }
   651	    }
   652	}
   653	
   654	// ============================================================================
   655	// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
   656	// ============================================================================
   657	
   658	void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
   659	    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
   660	
   661	    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   662	        for (size_t i = state.localBegin; i < state.localBegin + state.localCount; ++i) {
   663	            val_t sumB = ZERO;
   664	            const size_t localRow = (i - state.localBegin) * state.numTriangles;
   665	
   666	            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   667	                if (i == j) continue;
   668	
   669	                const uint16_t tauij = state.tau[localRow + j];
   670	
   671	                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
   672	                if (t < static_cast<size_t>(tauij)) continue;
   673	
   674	                const val_t coupling = state.coupling[localRow + j];
   675	                if (coupling <= ZERO) continue;
   676	
   677	                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
   678	                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
   679	                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];
   680	                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
   681	
   682	                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
   683	                sumB += coupling * radJ;
   684	            }
   685	
   686	            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
   687	            const val_t emission = (i == state.sourceIndex && t < state.numTimesteps / 2)
   688	                                       ? ONE : ZERO;
   689	            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + emission;
   690	        }
   691	
   692	        // Delays are strictly positive off the diagonal, so only the completed
   693	        // current row is needed before ranks can advance to the next timestep.
   694	        val_t* row = state.radB.data() + t * state.numTriangles;
   695	        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_FLOAT,
   696	                       row, state.recvCounts.data(), state.recvDisplacements.data(),
   697	                       MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   698	    }
   699	}
   700	
   701	// ============================================================================
   702	// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
   703	// ============================================================================
   704	
   705	void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
   706	    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
   707	
   708	    for (size_t i = state.localBegin; i < state.localBegin + state.localCount; ++i) {
   709	        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
   710	        int bestT = 0;
   711	
   712	        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
   713	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   714	            val_t sum = ZERO;
   715	
   716	            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
   717	                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];
   718	                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];
   719	                sum += pS * pB;
   720	            }
   721	
   722	            if (sum > maxCorr) {
   723	                maxCorr = sum;
   724	                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
   725	            }
   726	        }
   727	
   728	        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
   729	    }
   730	
   731	    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_FLOAT,
   732	                   state.distances.data(), state.recvCounts.data(),
   733	                   state.recvDisplacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   734	}
   735	
   736	// ============================================================================
   737	// Validation
   738	// ============================================================================
   739	
   740	bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
   741	    uint64_t globalNonZeroKij = 0;
   742	    MPI_Reduce(&state.localNonZeroKij, &globalNonZeroKij, 1, MPI_UINT64_T,
   743	               MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   744	
   745	    int valid = 1;
   750	
   751	    printf("\nValidation:\n");
   752	
   753	    // Check that distances are non-negative
   754	    bool allNonNegative = true;
   755	    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   756	    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
   757	    val_t sumDist = ZERO;
   758	    int nonZeroCount = 0;
   759	
   760	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   761	        val_t d = state.distances[i];
   762	        if (d < 0) {
   763	            allNonNegative = false;
   764	            printf("  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
   765	        }
   766	        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
   767	            printf("  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
   768	            valid = 0;
   769	        }
   770	        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
   771	        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
   772	        sumDist += d;
   773	        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;
   774	    }
   775	
   776	    printf("  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\n", minDist, maxDist);
   777	    printf("  Average distance: %.4f\n", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));
   778	    printf("  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\n", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);
   779	
   780	    // Check source distance is zero or very small
   781	    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];
   782	    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {
   783	        printf("  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\n", srcDist);
   784	    }
   785	
   786	    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)
   787	    int receivedEnergy = 0;
   788	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   789	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   790	            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {
   791	                receivedEnergy++;
   792	                break;
   793	            }
   794	        }
   795	    }
   796	
   797	    printf("  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\n", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);
   798	
   799	    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {
   800	        printf("  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\n");
   801	        valid = 0;
   802	    }
   803	
   804	    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)
   805	    const uint64_t totalKij = static_cast<uint64_t>(state.numTriangles) *
   806	                              state.numTriangles;
   807	    printf("  Non-zero form factors: %lu/%lu (%.2f%%)\n",
   808	           static_cast<unsigned long>(globalNonZeroKij),
   809	           static_cast<unsigned long>(totalKij),
   810	           100.0 * static_cast<double>(globalNonZeroKij) /
   811	               static_cast<double>(totalKij));
   812	
   813	    if (globalNonZeroKij == 0) {
   814	        printf("  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\n");
   815	        valid = 0;
   816	    }
   817	
   818	    if (!allNonNegative) {
   819	        valid = 0;
   820	    }
   821	
   822	    printf("  Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   823	    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   824	    return valid != 0;
   825	}
   826	
   827	// ============================================================================
   828	// Hash for Verification
   829	// ============================================================================
   830	
   831	uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
   832	    uint64_t hash = 0;
   833	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   834	        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);
   835	        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
   836	    }
   837	    return hash;
   838	}
   839	
   840	// ============================================================================
   841	// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
   842	// ============================================================================
   843	
   844	int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
   845	    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision
   846	    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480
   847	    int subdivisions = 0;
   848	    int triangles = 20;
   849	    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {
   850	        subdivisions++;
   851	        triangles *= 4;
   852	    }
   853	    return subdivisions;
   854	}
   855	
   856	// ============================================================================
   857	// Main
   858	// ============================================================================
   859	
   860	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   861	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   862	    printf("Options:\n");
   863	    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
   864	    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
   865	    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
   866	    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
   867	    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
   868	    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
   869	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   870	    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
   871	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   872	}
   873	
   874	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   875	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   876	
   877	    int rank = 0;
   878	    int worldSize = 1;
   879	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   880	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   881	
   882	    int targetTriangles = 320;
   883	    int timesteps = 50;
   884	    int sourceIdx = 0;
   885	    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
   886	    bool validate = false;
   887	    bool printResults = false;
   888	
   889	    // Parse command line arguments
   890	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   891	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   892	            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
   893	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   894	            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
   895	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   896	            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
   897	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   898	            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
   899	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   900	            validate = true;
   901	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
   902	            printResults = true;
   903	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   904	            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
   905	            MPI_Finalize();
   906	            return 0;
   907	        } else {
   908	            if (rank == 0) {
   909	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   910	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   911	            }
   912	            MPI_Finalize();
   913	            return 1;
   914	        }
   915	    }
   916	
   917	    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);
   918	
   919	    if (rank == 0) {
   920	        printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
   921	        printf("===================================\n");
   922	        printf("MPI processes: %d\n", worldSize);
   923	        printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
   924	        printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
   925	        printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
   926	        printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
   927	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   928	        printf("\n");
   929	    }
   930	
   931	    // Initialize
   932	    SimulationState state;
   933	    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
   934	                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity, rank, worldSize);
   935	
   936	    if (rank == 0) printf("\n");
   937	
   938	    // Precomputation
   939	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   940	    double phaseStart = MPI_Wtime();
   941	
   942	    computeTimeDelays(state);
   943	    computeFormFactors(state);
   944	
   945	    double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - phaseStart;
   946	    double preSeconds = 0.0;
   947	    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &preSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   948	    const long preDuration = static_cast<long>(preSeconds * 1000.0);
   949	
   950	    if (rank == 0) {

   952	        printf("\n");
   953	    }
   954	
   955	    // Simulation
   956	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   957	    phaseStart = MPI_Wtime();

   959	    runSimulation(state);
   960	
   961	    localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - phaseStart;
   962	    double simSeconds = 0.0;
   963	    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &simSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   964	    const long simDuration = static_cast<long>(simSeconds * 1000.0);
   965	
   966	    if (rank == 0) {
   967	        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", simDuration);
   968	        printf("\n");
   969	    }
   970	
   971	    // Distance computation
   972	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   973	    phaseStart = MPI_Wtime();
   974	
   975	    computeDistances(state);
   976	
   977	    localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - phaseStart;
   978	    double distSeconds = 0.0;
   979	    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &distSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   980	    const long distDuration = static_cast<long>(distSeconds * 1000.0);
   981	
   982	    if (rank == 0) {
   983	        printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n", distDuration);
   984	        printf("\n");
   985	    }
   986	
   987	    // Total time
   988	    const long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
   989	    if (rank == 0) printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);
   990	
   991	    // Performance metrics
   992	    size_t n = state.numTriangles;
   993	    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
   994	    const double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n) * n;
   995	    const double simOps = kijOps * t;
   996	    const double distOps = static_cast<double>(n) * t * t;
   997	
   998	    const uint64_t localMemory =
   999	        static_cast<uint64_t>(state.localCount) * n *
  1000	            (sizeof(val_t) + sizeof(uint16_t)) +
  1001	        static_cast<uint64_t>(t) * n * sizeof(val_t) +
  1002	        static_cast<uint64_t>(n) * sizeof(val_t);
  1003	    uint64_t maxMemory = 0;
  1004	    uint64_t aggregateMemory = 0;
  1005	    MPI_Reduce(&localMemory, &maxMemory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  1006	    MPI_Reduce(&localMemory, &aggregateMemory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  1007	
  1008	    if (rank == 0) {
  1009	        printf("\nPerformance:\n");
  1010	        printf("  MPI processes: %d\n", worldSize);
  1011	        printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
  1012	        printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
  1013	        printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
  1014	        printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
  1015	        printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
  1016	        printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);
  1017	        printf("  Memory per rank (max): %.2f MB\n", maxMemory / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
  1018	        printf("  Aggregate MPI memory: %.2f MB\n", aggregateMemory / (1024.0 * 1024.0));

  1023	        printf("\n");
  1024	    }
  1025	    
  1026	    // Print results for external validation
  1027	    if (printResults && rank == 0) {

  1029	        std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());

  1036	            MPI_Finalize();
  1037	            return 1;
  1038	        }
  1039	    }

// ============================================================================

bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
    uint64_t globalNonZeroKij = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&state.localNonZeroKij, &globalNonZeroKij, 1, MPI_UINT64_T,
               MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    int valid = 1;
    if (state.rank != 0) {
        MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        return valid != 0;
    }

    printf("\nValidation:\n");

    }
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults && rank == 0) {
        // Convert distances to double for output
        std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());
        print_results(distData, "Distances");
    }

    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        if (!validateResults(state)) {
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 1;
        }
    }

    MPI_Finalize();
    return 0;
}

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism without adding files or renaming the `roomsim` executable.

Key changes:

- Balanced receiver-row decomposition for form factors, delays, propagation, and distance calculation.
- Distributed dense matrices with replicated radiosity history.
- In-place `MPI_Allgatherv` synchronization per timestep.
- Process-count-invariant RNG stream and identical original result hashes.
- Root-only reporting, collective validation, and maximum-rank timings.
- CMake now unconditionally requires and links MPI.

Files changed:

- [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/roomsim/roomsim.cpp:524)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt:7)

Verification:

- Original and MPI result hashes match across 1, 2, 3, 4, and 24 ranks.
- Validation passed, including more ranks than triangles.
- AddressSanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer passed.
- 1,280 triangles: 26.699 s on one rank versus 6.735 s on four ranks—3.96× speedup.
- Maximum per-rank state fell from 9.48 MB to 2.45 MB.

Run with:

```bash
cmake -S roomsim -B build
cmake --build build -j
mpiexec -n 4 ./build/roomsim -n 1280 -t 50 -v
```

tokens used
82,226
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism without adding files or renaming the `roomsim` executable.

Key changes:

- Balanced receiver-row decomposition for form factors, delays, propagation, and distance calculation.
- Distributed dense matrices with replicated radiosity history.
- In-place `MPI_Allgatherv` synchronization per timestep.
- Process-count-invariant RNG stream and identical original result hashes.
- Root-only reporting, collective validation, and maximum-rank timings.
- CMake now unconditionally requires and links MPI.

Files changed:

- [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/roomsim/roomsim.cpp:524)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt:7)

Verification:

- Original and MPI result hashes match across 1, 2, 3, 4, and 24 ranks.
- Validation passed, including more ranks than triangles.
- AddressSanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer passed.
- 1,280 triangles: 26.699 s on one rank versus 6.735 s on four ranks—3.96× speedup.
- Maximum per-rank state fell from 9.48 MB to 2.45 MB.

Run with:

```bash
cmake -S roomsim -B build
cmake --build build -j
mpiexec -n 4 ./build/roomsim -n 1280 -t 50 -v
```
